Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in wind theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 372 and the invariant I(A,B) = 335 mod 4.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the wind decomposition. By minimality, the element a^372 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 335-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the wind object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 660. The moduli space has dimension at most 842 with 4 connected components.
